1	// 实体基类与分桶管理器
2	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
3	import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
4	import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
5	
6	export abstract class Entity {
7	  id = 0;
8	  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
9	  w = 16; h = 16;
10	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
11	  dead = false;
12	  onGround = false;
13	  hitWall = false;
14	  hitHead = false;
15	  dropThrough = false;
16	
17	  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
18	  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;
19	
20	  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
21	  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
22	  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
23	    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	export class EntityManager {
28	  nextId = 1;
29	  enemies: Entity[] = [];
30	  drops: Entity[] = [];
31	  npcs: Entity[] = [];
32	  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
33	  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
34	  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）
35	
36	  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
37	    e.id = this.nextId++;
38	    this[bucket].push(e);
39	  }
40	
41	  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
42	    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
43	      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
44	    }
45	    this.enemies = this.enemies.filter((e) => !e.dead);
46	    this.drops = this.drops.filter((e) => !e.dead);
47	    this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((e) => !e.dead);
48	    this.projectiles = this.projectiles.filter((e) => !e.dead);
49	    this.props = this.props.filter((e) => !e.dead);
50	    this.critters = this.critters.filter((e) => !e.dead);
51	  }
52	
53	  clear() {
54	    this.enemies.length = 0;
55	    this.drops.length = 0;
56	    this.npcs.length = 0;
57	    this.projectiles.length = 0;
58	    this.props.length = 0;
59	    this.critters.length = 0;
60	  }
61	
62	  all(): Entity[] {
63	    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
64	  }
65	}
66	

```

1	// SoA 多通道平面数组存储。所有热点代码只碰 TypedArray。
2	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
3	
4	export class TileStore {
5	  readonly w: number;
6	  readonly h: number;
7	  readonly type: Uint16Array;
8	  readonly flags: Uint8Array; // bit0 active bit1 framedDecor占位(预留)
9	  readonly frameX: Uint16Array;
10	  readonly frameY: Uint16Array;
11	  readonly wall: Uint16Array; // 原版 wld 墙 id（0-366）
12	  readonly liquid: Uint8Array;   // 0-255 液量
13	  readonly liquidType: Uint8Array; // 0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜
14	  readonly half: Uint8Array;      // halfBrick 位（原版 Tile.cs bTileHeader bit1）：下半 8px 实心
15	  readonly slope: Uint8Array;     // 坡面 0-4（原版编码；渲染暂未支持，生成期少用）
16	  readonly wire: Uint8Array;      // 电路位（原版 Tile.cs sTileHeader）：bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动(inActive)
17	  // wire 位掩码常量（对应原版 Tile.cs:396-459 的 wire/wire2/wire3/wire4/actuator/inActive）
18	  static readonly WIRE_RED = 1;
19	  static readonly WIRE_BLUE = 2;
20	  static readonly WIRE_GREEN = 4;
21	  static readonly WIRE_YELLOW = 8;
22	  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATOR = 16;
23	  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATED = 32;
24	  static readonly WIRE_COLORS = 15; // 四色掩码
25	
26	  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
27	  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
28	
29	  constructor(w: number, h: number) {
30	    this.w = w; this.h = h;
31	    const n = w * h;
32	    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
33	    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
34	    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
35	    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
36	    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
37	    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
38	    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
39	    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
40	    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
41	    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
42	  }
43	
44	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
45	
46	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
47	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
48	  }
49	
50	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }
51	
52	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
53	    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
54	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
55	      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
56	  }
57	
58	  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
59	  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
60	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
61	  }
62	
63	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
64	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
65	    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
66	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
67	    return !!d && d.solid;
68	  }
69	
70	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
71	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
72	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
73	    return !!d && d.platform;
74	  }
75	
76	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
77	    this.listeners.push(fn);
78	  }
79	
80	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
81	   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
82	  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
83	  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
84	    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
85	  }
86	
87	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
88	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
89	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
90	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
91	    this.type[i] = type;
92	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
93	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
94	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
95	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
96	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
97	  }
98	
99	  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
100	  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
101	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
102	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
103	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
104	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
105	  }
106	
107	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
108	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
109	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
110	    this.slope[i] = s;
111	    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
112	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
113	  }
114	
115	  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
116	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
117	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
118	    this.type[i] = type;
119	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
120	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
121	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
122	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
123	  }
124	
125	  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
126	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
127	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
128	    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
129	      this.wall[i] = wall;
130	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
131	    }
132	  }
133	
134	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
135	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
136	    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
137	  }
138	
139	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
140	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
141	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
142	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
143	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
144	  }
145	
146	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
147	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
148	  }
149	
150	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
151	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
152	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
153	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
154	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
155	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
156	    this.wire[i] = b;
157	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
158	  }
159	
160	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
161	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
162	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
163	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
164	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
165	    this.liquid[i] = a;
166	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
167	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
168	  }
169	}
170	

```

1	// 掉落物实体：重力、落地、磁吸拾取
2	import { Entity } from './Entity';
3	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
4	import { GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED, MAGNET_RADIUS, PICKUP_DELAY, TILE } from '../core/constants';
5	import { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';
6	import { ITEM_DEFS } from '../data/items';
7	import type { Player } from './Player';
8	
9	export class ItemDrop extends Entity {
10	  w = 12; h = 12;
11	  itemId: number;
12	  stack: number;
13	  age = 0;
14	  bobPhase: number;
15	  /** 原版 Item.color（凝胶等掉落继承怪物色，逐像素乘法贴轮廓渲染） */
16	  color?: number[]; // [r, g, b, a]
17	
18	  constructor(x: number, y: number, itemId: number, stack = 1, vx = 0, vy = -2) {
19	    super();
20	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
21	    this.itemId = itemId;
22	    this.stack = stack;
23	    this.vx = vx;
24	    this.vy = vy;
25	    this.bobPhase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
26	  }
27	
28	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
29	    this.age++;
30	    // 寿命（10 分钟）
31	    if (this.age > 60 * 600) { this.dead = true; return; }
32	
33	    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
34	    let magnetized = false;
35	    if (player && !player.dead && this.age > PICKUP_DELAY) {
36	      const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
37	      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
38	      // 碰到身体直接拾取（AABB 重叠），避免中心点距离判定漏拾
39	      const touching =
40	        this.x < player.x + player.w && this.x + this.w > player.x &&
41	        this.y < player.y + player.h && this.y + this.h > player.y;
42	      if (touching || dist < TILE * 1.7) {
43	        const before = this.stack;
44	        const left = player.inv.add(this.itemId, this.stack);
45	        if (left === 0) {
46	          this.dead = true;
47	          game.notifyInventoryChanged();
48	          // 铜币拾取用专属音效，其余走通用拾取
49	          // 音量略低（0.75）：拾取与挖掘声同时触发时两者都可闻，不被 Grab 盖住
50	          game.playSfx(ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId]?.key === 'coin_copper' ? 'coin' : 'pickup', 0.75);
51	          const def = ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId];
52	          if (def) game.showPickupLabel(def.key);
53	          return;
54	        }
55	        if (left !== before) game.notifyInventoryChanged();
56	        this.stack = left;
57	      } else if (dist < MAGNET_RADIUS) {
58	        // 磁吸：近处强拉（平方衰减），确保能战胜重力
59	        magnetized = true;
60	        const f = Math.pow(1 - dist / MAGNET_RADIUS, 1.5);
61	        const pull = 0.35 + f * 1.1;
62	        this.vx += (dx / (dist || 1)) * pull;
63	        this.vy += (dy / (dist || 1)) * pull;
64	        this.vx = Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, this.vx));
65	        this.vy = Math.max(-6, Math.min(5, this.vy));
66	      }
67	    }
68	
69	    // 磁吸状态下大幅减重，保证能被吸向玩家
70	    const g = magnetized ? GRAVITY * 0.25 : GRAVITY * 0.7;
71	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + g, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
72	    this.vx *= 0.96;
73	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
74	    // 入水小水花声（掉落物轻，音量小）
75	    const st = game.world.store;
76	    const li = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))];
77	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
78	    this.inWater = li > 80;
79	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && this.vy > 1.2) game.playSfx('splash', 0.35);
80	    void ITEM_DEFS;
81	  }
82	
83	  inWater = false;
84	
85	  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
86	}
87	

```

1	// 世界门面：TileStore + 元数据 + 实体容器 + 昼夜时钟
2	import { TileStore } from './TileStore';
3	import { T, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
4	
5	export interface ChestData { x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; locked?: boolean; }
6	
7	export class Clock {
8	  timeOfDay: number = 0.3;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始上午 7:12 ≈ 0.3）
9	  dayCount: number = 1;
10	  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
11	  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
12	  tick(dtMs: number) {
13	    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
14	    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
15	  }
16	  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > 0.25 && this.timeOfDay < 0.75; }
17	  get dayFactor(): number {
18	    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
19	    const t = this.timeOfDay;
20	    if (t > 0.28 && t < 0.72) return 1;
21	    if (t >= 0.72 && t < 0.80) return 1 - (t - 0.72) / 0.08 * 0.68;
22	    if (t >= 0.80 || t < 0.20) return 0.32;
23	    return 0.32 + (t - 0.20) / 0.08 * 0.68;
24	  }
25	  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
26	}
27	
28	export class World {
29	  store: TileStore;
30	  name: string;
31	  seed: number;
32	  spawnX = 0; spawnY = 0;
33	  groundLevel = 0; rockLevel = 0;   // tile 坐标
34	  /** 地狱顶（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200；TerrainPass 设定，SceneMetrics/BGM/背景共用） */
35	  lavaLine = 0;
36	  clock: Clock;
37	  chests: ChestData[] = [];
38	  // Boss 进度旗标
39	  flags: Record<string, boolean> = { downedEyeOfCthulhu: false, shadowOrbSmashed: false, hardMode: false };
40	  // 树登记：砍树干时找到整棵树（roots → 范围）
41	  trees: Array<{ x: number; y: number; h: number }> = [];
42	  /** 战争迷雾：1 = 已探索。按 tile 粒度。 */
43	  explored: Uint8Array;
44	  /** 原版树样式数据（header treeX/treeStyle）：横向 4 区森林树冠样式 */
45	  treeX: number[] = [];
46	  treeStyle: number[] = [0, 0, 0, 0];
47	  /** 世界级生物群系常量(原版 header,生成期 Reset pass 掷出) */
48	  crimson = false;          // true=猩红 false=腐化
49	  dungeonX = 0;            // 地牢位置
50	  jungleX = 0;             // 丛林位置
51	  /** TreeTops 13 区域变体（v≥211 wld 权威；索引 0-3 森林/5 丛林/6 雪/7 神圣） */
52	  treeTops: number[] = [];
53	
54	  exploredVersion = 0;
55	  markExplored(cx: number, cy: number, radius: number) {
56	    this.exploredVersion++;
57	    const st = this.store;
58	    const x0 = Math.max(0, cx - radius), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, cx + radius);
59	    const y0 = Math.max(0, cy - radius), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, cy + radius);
60	    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
61	      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
62	        if ((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 <= radius * radius) {
63	          this.explored[y * st.w + x] = 1;
64	        }
65	      }
66	    }
67	  }
68	
69	  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界') {
70	    this.store = new TileStore(w, h);
71	    this.explored = new Uint8Array(w * h);
72	    this.seed = seed;
73	    this.name = name;
74	    // 1 游戏日 = 30 现实分钟（24→40 后折中）
75	    this.clock = new Clock(30 * 60 * 1000);
76	  }
77	
78	  get w() { return this.store.w; }
79	  get h() { return this.store.h; }
80	  get tiles() { return this.store; }
81	
82	  /** 破坏一个 tile（含 framed 物体的锚点处理由调用方负责），返回掉落 item key */
83	  breakTileAt(x: number, y: number): string | null {
84	    const t = this.store.get(x, y);
85	    if (t === 0) return null;
86	    const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
87	    this.store.setTile(x, y, 0);
88	    // v_ 图块无显式 drop → 回退同名放置物品(阶段5 自动注册)
89	    return d.drop ?? (d.key.startsWith('v_') ? 'place_' + d.key : null);
90	  }
91	
92	  isSolidPx(px: number, py: number): boolean {
93	    return this.store.isSolid(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16));
94	  }
95	
96	  /** 液体量（0-255）按像素坐标 */
97	  liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
98	    return this.store.liquid[this.store.idx(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16))];
99	  }
100	
101	  registerTree(x: number, y: number, h: number) {
102	    this.trees.push({ x, y, h });
103	  }
104	}
105	
106	export { T };
107

1	// 音效：原版 wav 素材（public/sounds/）+ WebAudio 合成兜底
2	// hit 敌人受击 / hurt 玩家受击 / pickup 拾取 / dig 挖掘 / place 放置 / drink 饮用 / chop 砍树 / splash 落水
3	// + killed 怪物死亡 / zombie 僵尸环境声 / roar Boss咆哮 / coin 拾币 / door_open door_close / tink 敲石 / shatter 玻璃破碎 / drown 溺水
4	export type SfxName =
5	  | 'hit' | 'hurt' | 'pickup' | 'dig' | 'place' | 'drink' | 'chop' | 'splash'
6	  | 'killed' | 'pkilled' | 'zombie' | 'roar' | 'coin' | 'door_open' | 'door_close' | 'tink' | 'shatter' | 'drown'
7	  | 'menuOpen' | 'menuClose' | 'menuTick';
8	
9	// 逻辑名 → 原版 wav 文件（多文件随机选 + 音高扰动）
10	const WAV_MAP: Record<SfxName, string[]> = {
11	  hit: ['NPC_Hit_1', 'NPC_Hit_2', 'NPC_Hit_3', 'NPC_Hit_4', 'NPC_Hit_5'],
12	  hurt: ['Player_Hit_0', 'Player_Hit_1', 'Player_Hit_2'],
13	  killed: ['NPC_Killed_1', 'NPC_Killed_2', 'NPC_Killed_3'],
14	  pkilled: ['Player_Killed'],
15	  zombie: ['Zombie_1', 'Zombie_2', 'Zombie_3', 'Zombie_4', 'Zombie_5', 'Zombie_6'],
16	  roar: ['Roar_0', 'Roar_1'],
17	  pickup: ['Grab'],
18	  dig: ['Dig_0', 'Dig_1', 'Dig_2'],
19	  chop: ['Grass'],
20	  tink: ['Tink_0', 'Tink_1'],
21	  place: ['Dig_1', 'Dig_2'],
22	  shatter: ['Shatter'],
23	  coin: ['Coin_0', 'Coin_1', 'Coins'],
24	  door_open: ['Door_Opened'],
25	  door_close: ['Door_Closed'],
26	  splash: ['Splash_0', 'Splash_1'],
27	  drown: ['Drown'],
28	  // 菜单 UI（原版 id 10=Menu_Open / id 11=Menu_Close / id 12=Menu_Tick）
29	  menuOpen: ['Menu_Open'],
30	  menuClose: ['Menu_Close'],
31	  menuTick: ['Menu_Tick'],
32	  // 以下无原版素材，走合成
33	  drink: [],
34	};
35	
36	export class Sfx {
37	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
38	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
39	  muted = false;
40	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
41	  master = 1;
42	
43	  private extraFiles = new Set<string>();
44	  /** 追加加载额外 wav 文件（怪物专属声效等） */
45	  addFiles(files: string[]) {
46	    for (const f of files) this.extraFiles.add(f);
47	    const ctx = this.ctx;
48	    if (!ctx) return;
49	    for (const f of files) {
50	      if (this.buffers.has(f)) continue;
51	      fetch(`sounds/${f}.wav`).then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null)).then((ab) => {
52	        if (!ab) return;
53	        ctx.decodeAudioData(ab).then((buf) => this.buffers.set(f, buf)).catch(() => {});
54	      }).catch(() => {});
55	    }
56	  }
57	
58	  /** 预加载全部 wav（在用户手势后调用可获得最佳兼容；挂起态 decodeAudioData 也可用） */
59	  async load(): Promise<void> {
60	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
61	    if (!ctx) return;
62	    const files = new Set<string>();
63	    for (const list of Object.values(WAV_MAP)) for (const f of list) files.add(f);
64	    for (const f of this.extraFiles) files.add(f);
65	    await Promise.all([...files].map(async (f) => {
66	      try {
67	        const r = await fetch(`sounds/${f}.wav`);
68	        if (!r.ok) return;
69	        const buf = await ctx.decodeAudioData(await r.arrayBuffer());
70	        this.buffers.set(f, buf);
71	      } catch { /* 单文件失败静默 */ }
72	    }));
73	  }
74	
75	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
76	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
77	    if (!this.ctx) {
78	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
79	      if (!AC) return null;
80	      this.ctx = new AC();
81	    }
82	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
83	    return this.ctx;
84	  }
85	
86	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
87	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1): boolean {
88	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
89	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
90	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol);
91	  }
92	
93	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
94	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1): boolean {
95	    if (!files.length) return false;
96	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol);
97	  }
98	
99	  /** 按文件名直播 */
100	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1): boolean {
101	    const ctx = this.ensure();
102	    if (!ctx) return false;
103	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
104	    if (!buf) return false;
105	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
106	    src.buffer = buf;
107	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
108	    const g = ctx.createGain();
109	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
110	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
111	    src.start();
112	    return true;
113	  }
114	
115	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1) {
116	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）
117	    if (this.playWav(name, vol)) return;
118	    const ctx = this.ensure();
119	    if (!ctx) return;
120	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
121	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
122	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
123	      const g = ctx.createGain();
124	      o.type = type;
125	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
126	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
127	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
128	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
129	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
130	      o.start(t + delay);
131	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
132	    };
133	    switch (name) {
134	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
135	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
136	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
137	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
138	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
139	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
140	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
141	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
142	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
143	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;

1	// 自有存档格式：JSON 外壳（人可读）+ RLE 二进制 tile 段（base64）
2	import type { World } from '../world/World';
3	import { World as WorldClass } from '../world/World';
4	import type { Player } from '../entities/Player';
5	
6	// ---- varint / base64 工具 ----
7	function writeVarint(out: number[], v: number) {
8	  v = v < 0 ? 0 : v;
9	  while (v >= 0x80) {
10	    out.push((v & 0x7f) | 0x80);
11	    v >>>= 7;
12	  }
13	  out.push(v);
14	}
15	
16	export function bytesToB64(bytes: number[]): string {
17	  const u8 = new Uint8Array(bytes);
18	  let s = '';
19	  for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(u8[i]);
20	  return btoa(s);
21	}
22	
23	function b64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
24	  const s = atob(b64);
25	  const u8 = new Uint8Array(s.length);
26	  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u8[i] = s.charCodeAt(i);
27	  return u8;
28	}
29	
30	function readVarint(u8: Uint8Array, pos: { i: number }): number {
31	  let v = 0, shift = 0;
32	  while (pos.i < u8.length) {
33	    const b = u8[pos.i++];
34	    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
35	    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
36	    shift += 7;
37	  }
38	  return v;
39	}
40	
41	// ---- 正式 RLE 格式 ----
42	// tiles 每条目: varint(run-1) varint(type) [varint(frameX) varint(frameY) 仅当 type!=0]
43	// walls/liquid: varint(run-1) varint(value)
44	
45	export interface SaveData {
46	  format: 'sandboxworld.save';
47	  version: number;
48	  header: {
49	    name: string; seed: number; width: number; height: number;
50	    spawn: [number, number]; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine?: number;
51	    crimson?: boolean; dungeonX?: number; jungleX?: number;
52	    treeX?: number[]; treeStyle?: number[]; treeTops?: number[];
53	    created: number; playTimeMs: number;
54	    wldVersion?: number;
55	  };
56	  clock: { timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number };
57	  tiles: string;   // base64 RLE
58	  walls: string;
59	  liquid: string;
60	  liquidType: string;  // base64 RLE（0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜）
61	  wire?: string;       // base64 RLE 电路位（bit0-3 四色线 bit4 致动器 bit5 已致动；旧档缺此字段=全 0）
62	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>;
63	  player: {
64	    hp: number; x: number; y: number;
65	    inventory: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>;
66	    selected: number;
67	  };
68	  npcs: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null }>;
69	  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
70	}
71	
72	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): number[] {
73	  const out: number[] = [];
74	  const n = values.length;
75	  let i = 0;
76	  while (i < n) {
77	    const v = values[i];
78	    let run = 1;
79	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
80	    writeVarint(out, run - 1);
81	    writeVarint(out, v);
82	    i += run;
83	  }
84	  return out;
85	}
86	
87	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }): number[] {
88	  const out: number[] = [];
89	  const n = st.type.length;
90	  let i = 0;
91	  while (i < n) {
92	    const t = st.type[i], fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
93	    let run = 1;
94	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === t && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
95	    writeVarint(out, run - 1);
96	    writeVarint(out, t);
97	    if (t !== 0) {
98	      writeVarint(out, fx);
99	      writeVarint(out, fy);
100	    }
101	    i += run;
102	  }
103	  return out;
104	}
105	
106	export function saveGame(world: World, player: Player, playTimeMs: number): string {
107	  const st = world.store;
108	  // 幽灵净化:存档格式不携带 active 位(加载时 type≠0 即激活),
109	  // 非活性格上的残留 type 必须归零,否则重读后全部变成浮空实体块
110	  for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
111	    if (!st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) st.type[i] = 0;
112	  }
113	  const data: SaveData = {
114	    format: 'sandboxworld.save',
115	    version: 2,
116	    header: {
117	      name: world.name, seed: world.seed, width: st.w, height: st.h,
118	      spawn: [world.spawnX, world.spawnY], groundLevel: world.groundLevel, rockLevel: world.rockLevel,
119	      lavaLine: world.lavaLine,
120	      treeX: world.treeX, treeStyle: world.treeStyle, treeTops: world.treeTops,
121	      crimson: world.crimson, dungeonX: world.dungeonX, jungleX: world.jungleX,
122	      created: Date.now(), playTimeMs,
123	    },
124	    clock: { timeOfDay: world.clock.timeOfDay, dayCount: world.clock.dayCount },
125	    tiles: bytesToB64(rleTiles(st)),
126	    walls: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wall)),
127	    liquid: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquid)),
128	    liquidType: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquidType)),
129	    wire: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wire)),
130	    chests: world.chests,
131	    player: {
132	      hp: player.hp, x: player.x, y: player.y,
133	      inventory: player.inv.slots, selected: player.inv.selected,
134	    },
135	    npcs: [],
136	    flags: world.flags,
137	  };
138	  return JSON.stringify(data);
139	}
140	
141	export function loadSave(json: string): { world: World; player: { hp: number; x: number; y: number; inventory: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; selected: number } } {
142	  const data = JSON.parse(json) as SaveData;
143	  const world = new WorldClass(data.header.width, data.header.height, data.header.seed, data.header.name);
144	  world.spawnX = data.header.spawn[0];
145	  world.spawnY = data.header.spawn[1];
146	  world.groundLevel = data.header.groundLevel;
147	  world.rockLevel = data.header.rockLevel;
148	  // 旧档缺 lavaLine（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200）
149	  world.lavaLine = data.header.lavaLine ?? data.header.height - 200;
150	  if (data.header.treeX) world.treeX = data.header.treeX;
151	  if (data.header.treeStyle) world.treeStyle = data.header.treeStyle;
152	  if (data.header.treeTops?.length) world.treeTops = data.header.treeTops;
153	  if (data.header.crimson !== undefined) world.crimson = data.header.crimson;
154	  if (data.header.dungeonX !== undefined) world.dungeonX = data.header.dungeonX;
155	  if (data.header.jungleX !== undefined) world.jungleX = data.header.jungleX;
156	  world.clock.timeOfDay = data.clock.timeOfDay;
157	  world.clock.dayCount = data.clock.dayCount;
158	  world.chests = data.chests;
159	  world.flags = data.flags;
160	
161	  const st = world.store;
162	  // tiles
163	  {
164	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.tiles);
165	    const pos = { i: 0 };
166	    let i = 0;
167	    while (i < st.type.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
168	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
169	      const t = readVarint(u8, pos);
170	      let fx = 0, fy = 0;
171	      if (t !== 0) {
172	        fx = readVarint(u8, pos);
173	        fy = readVarint(u8, pos);
174	      }
175	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.type.length; k++, i++) {
176	        st.type[i] = t; st.flags[i] = t !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
177	        st.frameX[i] = fx; st.frameY[i] = fy;
178	      }
179	    }
180	  }
181	  // walls（version<2 的旧档墙值迁移：1=土→2, 2=石→1, 3=木→4（现为原版 id））
182	  {
183	    const legacy = (data.version ?? 1) < 2;
184	    const migrate = (v: number) => (legacy ? (v === 1 ? 2 : v === 2 ? 1 : v === 3 ? 4 : v) : v);
185	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.walls);
186	    const pos = { i: 0 };
187	    let i = 0;
188	    while (i < st.wall.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
189	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
190	      const v = migrate(readVarint(u8, pos));
191	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wall.length; k++, i++) st.wall[i] = v;
192	    }
193	  }
194	  // wire 电路位（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0 = 无导线，缺字段容忍同 liquidType）
195	  {
196	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.wire ?? '');
197	    const pos = { i: 0 };
198	    let i = 0;
199	    while (i < st.wire.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
200	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
201	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
202	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wire.length; k++, i++) st.wire[i] = v;
203	    }
204	  }
205	  // liquid type（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0，由液体模拟按 1=水 补全）
206	  {
207	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquidType ?? '');
208	    const pos = { i: 0 };
209	    let i = 0;
210	    while (i < st.liquidType.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
211	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
212	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
213	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquidType.length; k++, i++) st.liquidType[i] = v;
214	    }
215	  }
216	  // liquid
217	  {
218	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquid);
219	    const pos = { i: 0 };
220	    let i = 0;
221	    while (i < st.liquid.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
222	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
223	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
224	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquid.length; k++, i++) st.liquid[i] = v;
225	    }
226	  }
227	  return { world, player: data.player };
228	}
229

122	      const n = def.maxStack === 1 ? 1 : Math.min(def.maxStack, 99);
123	      const left = p.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[def.key], n);
124	      if (left > 0) overflow.push([def.key, left]);
125	    }

127	    const sx = this.world.spawnX, sy = this.world.spawnY;
128	    const x0 = sx + 6, x1 = sx + 70, yBot = sy - 2, yTop = sy - 40;
129	    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
130	      for (let y = yTop; y <= yBot; y++) {
131	        st.setTile(x, y, 0);
132	        st.setLiquid(x, y, 0, 0);
133	      }
134	      st.setTile(x, yBot, T.STONE);
135	      st.setTile(x, yBot + 1, T.STONE);
136	    }
137	    // 收集可放置 tile（有物品指向，去重）
138	    const placeable: number[] = [];
139	    const seen = new Set<number>();
140	    for (const def of ITEM_DEFS) {
141	      if (!def.tile) continue;
142	      const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[def.tile];
143	      if (tid === undefined || tid === 0 || seen.has(tid)) continue;
144	      seen.add(tid);
145	      placeable.push(tid);
146	    }
147	    // 逐行摆放：行高 7（最高家具 5 格 + 间隙），每行放满换行并铺新地板
148	    let cx = x0 + 1, cy = yBot - 1;
149	    const rowH = 7;
150	    for (const tid of placeable) {
151	      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
152	      const w = td.vanilla?.fw ?? td.w ?? 1;
153	      const h = td.vanilla?.fh ?? td.h ?? 1;
154	      if (cx + w > x1 - 1) {
155	        cx = x0 + 1;
156	        cy -= rowH;
157	        for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) st.setTile(x, cy, T.STONE);
158	      }
159	      for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
160	        for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
161	          // 原版帧语义（TileObject.Place）：顶行 frameY=0，底行最大——
162	          // 此处 cy 是底部行，dy=0 是底行 → frameY = (h-1-dy)*18
163	          st.setTile(cx + dx, cy - dy, tid, td.vanilla ? dx * 18 : dx, td.vanilla ? (h - 1 - dy) * 18 : dy);
164	        }
165	      }
166	      cx += w + 1;
167	    }
168	    // ---- 3) 溢出道具以掉落物排在展示区上方（背包 40 格装不下的部分） ----
169	    let dxDrop = x0;
170	    let dyDrop = yTop + 3;
171	    for (const [key, n] of overflow) {
172	      this.spawnDrop(dxDrop * TILE + 8, dyDrop * TILE, key, n, 0, 0);
173	      dxDrop += 2;
174	      if (dxDrop > x1 - 1) { dxDrop = x0; dyDrop += 3; }
175	    }
176	    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
177	    this.cb.onToast(`开发者模式：${overflow.length} 种道具背包装不下，已排在展示区上方；全部可放置图块在出生点右侧`);
178	  }

224	  constructor(root: HTMLElement, cb: GameCallbacks, atlas?: SpriteAtlas | null) {
225	    this.assets = buildAssets();
226	    if (atlas) {
227	      this.atlas = atlas;
228	      this.autotiler = new AutoTiler(atlas);
229	    }
230	    this.renderer = new Renderer(this.assets, atlas);
231	    this.renderer.attach(root);
232	    this.sfx.load(); // 原版 wav 预解码（挂起态也可解码）
233	    // 怪物专属声效文件追加载入
234	    const extra = new Set<string>();
235	    for (const d of Object.values(ENEMY_DEFS)) for (const f of [...d.hitSound, ...d.killedSound]) extra.add(f);
236	    this.sfx.addFiles([...extra]);
237	    this.input = new Input(this.renderer.canvas);
238	    this.cb = cb;
239	    // 快捷栏数字键：事件驱动（避免单帧内 down+up 被轮询漏掉）
240	    this.input.keydownHandlers.push((code) => {
241	      if (this.player && /^Digit[0-9]$/.test(code)) {
242	        this.player.inv.selected = code === 'Digit0' ? 9 : Number(code.slice(5)) - 1;
243	        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
244	      }
245	      // 缩放调节
246	      if (code === 'Equal' || code === 'NumpadAdd') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget + 0.15);
247	      if (code === 'Minus' || code === 'NumpadSubtract') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget - 0.15);
248	      // R:五彩扳手/宏伟蓝图模式循环(红蓝绿黄→剪线→致动器→剪致动器)
249	      if (code === 'KeyR') {
250	        const held = this.player?.inv.heldItem();
251	        if (held && ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.wireTool && (ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.key === 'multicolor_wrench' || ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.key === 'grand_design')) {
252	          const modes = [
253	            [TOOL_RED, TOOL_GREEN, TOOL_BLUE, TOOL_YELLOW],
254	            [TOOL_CUTTER, TOOL_RED, TOOL_GREEN, TOOL_BLUE, TOOL_YELLOW],
255	            [TOOL_ACTUATOR],
256	            [TOOL_CUTTER, TOOL_ACTUATOR],
257	          ];
258	          const cur = modes.findIndex((m) => m.reduce((a, b) => a | b, 0) === this.wireToolMode);
259	          const next = modes[(cur + 1) % modes.length].reduce((a, b) => a | b, 0);
260	          this.wireToolMode = next;
261	          const name = next & TOOL_CUTTER ? (next & TOOL_ACTUATOR ? '剪致动器' : '剪线') : next & TOOL_ACTUATOR ? '致动器' : '四色铺线';
262	          this.cb.onToast(`工具模式:${name}`);
263	        }
264	      }
265	    });
266	  }
267	
268	  // ================= 生命周期 =================
269	
270	  async newWorld(seedText: string, width: number, height: number, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void, opts?: { name?: string; evil?: -1 | 0 | 1; onWorldPartial?: (world: import('../world/World').World, passIndex: number, passName: string) => void }) {
271	    this.world = await generateWorld({ width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, onWorldPartial: opts?.onWorldPartial }, (label, p) => onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7));
272	    // 水体预沉降：创建世界时全图激活+步进至稳定，带进度条
273	    onProgress?.('水体沉降', 0.72);
274	    await this.settleLiquids((p) => onProgress?.('水体沉降', 0.72 + p * 0.27));
275	    onProgress?.('完成', 1);
276	    this.afterWorldLoad();
277	    this.cb.onWorldReady();
278	  }
279	
280	  /** 全图液体沉降：原版 WorldFile.cs:365-389 读档时序 1:1 移植。
281	   *  QuickWater 自底向上直接搬运 → WaterCheck 重建活动列表 →
282	   *  quickSettle 循环 UpdateLiquid 至收敛 → 复位后再次 WaterCheck。
283	   *  收敛后世界液体即达最终静止状态，进游戏不再有"靠近才流动" */
284	  private async settleLiquids(onProgress?: (p: number) => void) {
285	    const sim = new LiquidSim(this.world);
286	    onProgress?.(0.05);
287	    sim.quickWater();
288	    onProgress?.(0.35);
289	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
290	    sim.waterCheck();
291	    sim.quickSettle = true;
292	    const MAX_ITER = 100000; // 原版 WorldFile 同上限
293	    let iter = 0;
294	    while (sim.numLiquid > 0 && iter < MAX_ITER) {
295	      sim.updateLiquid();
296	      iter++;
297	      if (iter % 500 === 0) {
298	        onProgress?.(0.35 + Math.min(0.6, iter / 20000));
299	        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
300	      }
301	    }
302	    sim.quickSettle = false;
303	    sim.waterCheck();
304	    sim.dispose(); // 丢弃临时模拟器（注销监听，避免泄漏到运行期）
305	    onProgress?.(1);
306	  }
307	
308	  async loadWorld(world: World, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void) {
309	    this.world = world;
310	    onProgress?.('水体沉降', 0.5);
311	    await this.settleLiquids((p) => onProgress?.('水体沉降', p));
312	    this.afterWorldLoad();
313	    this.cb.onWorldReady();
314	  }
315	
316	  private afterWorldLoad() {
317	    const w = this.world;
318	    // 原版墙渲染器（TEdit WallFraming 移植；墙 id 直用 wld id，贴图 Wall_N.png）
319	    const wallTiler = this.atlas ? new VanillaWallTiler(this.atlas) : null;
320	    this.chunks = new ChunkCache(w, this.assets.tileSheets, this.autotiler, wallTiler);
321	    // 电路系统(Wiring.cs 移植):器件表 + 触发源登记(测重板 428/感应器 423)
322	    this.wiring = new Wiring(w.store);
323	    this.wiring.worldSurface = w.groundLevel;
324	    attachDevices(this.wiring, this.makeDeviceCtx());

409	
410	  private fixedUpdate(dt: number) {
411	    this.tickCount++;
412	    // 原版 HitTile 衰减（每 2 tick 一次——原版按帧衰减配原版 ~27 帧挥速；
413	    // 我们的挥击间隔更长，等比减半保持"连击可积累、停手会消退"）
414	    if (this.tickCount % 2 === 0) this.hitTiles.update();
415	    const w = this.world;
416	    w.clock.tick(dt * 1000);
417	    // 昼夜切换：提示 + BGM 变奏
418	    const isDay = w.clock.isDay;
419	    if (isDay !== this.lastWasDay) {
420	      if (this.lastWasDay !== null) {
421	        this.cb.onToast(isDay ? '☀ 太阳升起来了，新的一天开始了' : '🌙 夜幕降临，小心出没的怪物…');
422	        this.cb.onDayNight?.(isDay);
423	        // 昼/夜逻辑感应器(423)触发
424	        this.triggerDayNightSensors();
425	      }
426	      this.lastWasDay = isDay;
427	    }
428	    // ---- 原版环境采样 + BGM 选曲（SceneMetrics/UpdateAudio_DecideOnNewMusic；15 tick 刷新） ----
429	    if (this.tickCount % 15 === 0) {
430	      const townNPCCount = this.entities.npcs.filter((n) => !(n as TownNPC).dead).length;
431	      this.scene = scanScene(w, this.player.cx, this.player.cy, townNPCCount);
432	      let bossNearby = false;
433	      for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
434	        const en = e as unknown as { x: number; y: number; def?: { boss?: boolean } };
435	        if (en.def?.boss && Math.abs(en.x - this.player.cx) < 5000 && Math.abs(en.y - this.player.cy) < 5000) { bossNearby = true; break; }
436	      }
437	      const music = pickMusic({
438	        scene: this.scene, isDay, bossNearby,
439	        worldSurface: w.groundLevel, rockLevel: w.rockLevel, lavaLine: w.lavaLine || w.store.h - 200,
440	        maxTilesX: w.store.w, centerX: this.player.cx, centerY: this.player.cy, ocean: this.scene.zoneBeach,
441	        state: this.musicState,
442	      });
443	      if (music !== this.lastMusic) {
444	        this.lastMusic = music;
445	        this.cb.onMusic?.(music);
446	      }
447	    }

450	    // ---- 玩家输入 ----
451	    const inp = this.input;
452	    // 点击右上角小地图 → 开关全屏地图
453	    if (inp.mouseDown && !this._mapClickLatch) {

457	      if (!fm.open && inMap && !inp.uiBlocking) {

461	        fm.zoom = 0.5;
462	        fm.zoomTarget = 2;
463	        fm.anchorU = player.cx / TILE;

466	        fm.anchorMY = this.renderer.canvas.height / 2;

468	        fm.panX = (this.world.w * fm.zoom) / 2 - fm.anchorU * fm.zoom;
469	        fm.panY = (this.world.h * fm.zoom) / 2 - fm.anchorV * fm.zoom;
470	        this.renderer['_mapInit'] = false;
471	        // 打开地图的这次点击不算拖拽（否则按住期间 drawFullMap 会把锚点重设到点击处，飞向主角变飞向点击处）

479	    if (this.renderer.fullMap.open && !inp.uiBlocking) {

483	        this._mapPressY = inp.mouseY;

492	          const cx0 = viewW / 2 - (this.world.w * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panX;

56	function flipBlock(
57	  w: Wiring, x: number, y: number,
58	  blockW: number, blockH: number, tilesW: number, tilesH: number,
59	  axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number, delta: number,
60	): void {
61	  const st = w.st;
62	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
63	  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % blockW / 18);
64	  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % blockH / 18);
65	  let d = delta;
66	  const ai = st.idx(ox, oy);
67	  if (axis === 'y' ? st.frameY[ai] >= threshold : st.frameX[ai] >= threshold) d = -delta;
68	  for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + tilesW; ix++) {
69	    for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + tilesH; iy++) {
70	      if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
71	      const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
72	      w.skipCell(ix, iy);
73	      if (axis === 'y') st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii] + d);
74	      else st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii] + d, st.frameY[ii]);
75	    }
76	  }
77	}

19	  key: 'v_placeholder', name: '?', hp: 1, damage: 0, knockbackResist: 0.5,
20	  width: 16, height: 16, mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
21	  hitSound: ['NPC_Hit_1'], killedSound: ['NPC_Killed_1'], drops: [],
22	};
23	
24	export class Enemy extends Entity {
25	  /** 原版 NPC id（数据驱动路径启用时非空） */
26	  vanillaId: number | null = null;
27	  vanilla: VanillaNpc | null = null;
28	  // ---- 蠕虫多段体（AI_006，NPC.cs:18046）：头 aiStyle 6，编号约定 头+1=身 头+2=尾 ----
29	  /** 链上紧随本段的一段（头 → 身×n → 尾） */
30	  wormNext: Enemy | null = null;
31	  /** 本段跟随的前一段（非空 = 本段是身体段，跳过 AI 只做跟随） */
32	  wormFollow: Enemy | null = null;
33	  /** 上一 tick 位置（段跟随用：段复制前一段的旧位置 = 经典贪吃蛇链） */
34	  prevX = 0; prevY = 0;
35	
36	  /** AI_006 头部（L18645 通用常数 maxSpd=8 accel=0.07；穿墙直行；段链跟随） */
37	  private wormAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
38	    const maxSpd = 8, accel = 0.07;
39	    // 朝向：有玩家朝玩家，无玩家缓慢巡游
40	    let dx: number, dy: number;
41	    if (player) { dx = player.cx - this.cx; dy = player.cy - this.cy; }
42	    else { dx = Math.cos(this.aiT * 0.02) * 10; dy = Math.sin(this.aiT * 0.013) * 10; }
43	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
44	    this.vx += (dx / d) * accel;
45	    this.vy += (dy / d) * accel;
46	    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
47	    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
48	    this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
49	    // 蠕虫穿墙：直接位移（原版 noTileCollide）
50	    this.x += this.vx;
51	    this.y += this.vy;
52	    // 段链跟随（原版 L52271-52308）：方向向量收缩维持 linkDist 间距——
53	    // shrink = (dist - linkDist)/dist；position += dxC*shrink（原版 num63/num64）
54	    for (let s = this.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
55	      const fx = s.wormFollow!;
56	      const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
57	      const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
58	      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
59	      if (dist > 0.01) {
60	        const linkDist = s.w;               // 原版 num64 = width
61	        const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
62	        s.x += dxC * shrink;
63	        s.y += dyC * shrink;
64	        s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;         // 原版 spriteDirection（L52305）
65	      }
66	    }
67	  }
68	
69	  /** 由头生成段链（原版各 worm 的 NewNPC 链，NPC.cs:18174+）：body×n + tail */
70	  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
71	    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
72	    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
73	    let prev = head;
74	    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
75	      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
76	      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
77	      if (!s) continue;
78	      s.wormFollow = prev;
79	      prev.wormNext = s;
80	      prev = s;
81	      segs.push(s);
82	    }
83	    return segs;
84	  }
85	
86	
87	  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
88	  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
89	    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
90	    if (!v) return null;
91	    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
92	    e.vanillaId = id;
93	    e.vanilla = v;
94	    const hit = vanillaSoundName(v.HitSound) ?? 'NPC_Hit_1';
95	    const kill = vanillaSoundName(v.DeathSound) ?? 'NPC_Killed_1';
96	    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
97	    e.def = {
98	      ...e.def,
99	      name: v.name, hp: v.lifeMax, damage: v.damage, defense: v.defense,
100	      // 原版 knockBackResist 是"承受击退的比例"（0.5=吃一半）；本仓库语义是
101	      // "抗性"（hurt(): resist<0.9 才生效，kbx*(1-resist)）→ 换算 1-比例
102	      knockbackResist: Math.max(0, Math.min(0.89, 1 - (v.knockBackResist ?? 0.5))),
103	      width: v.width, height: v.height, flying,
104	      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),
105	      nightOnly: v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5, underground: false,
106	      mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
107	      hitSound: [hit], killedSound: [kill], drops: v.critter ? [] : vanillaNpcDrops(id),
108	      // 小动物：无接触伤害、不夜行
109	      ...(v.critter ? { damage: 0, nightOnly: false } : {}),
110	    };
111	    e.hp = v.lifeMax;
112	    e.maxHp = v.lifeMax;
113	    e.w = v.width;
114	    e.h = v.height;
115	    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
116	    e.colorRGBA = v.color ? [v.color[0], v.color[1], v.color[2], v.color[3] ?? 255] : null; // 原版 color 字段
117	    e.x = x - e.w / 2;
118	    e.y = y - e.h / 2;
119	    return e;
120	  }
121	
122	  def: EnemyDef;
123	  hp: number;
124	  maxHp: number;
125	  iframes = 0;
126	  animT = 0;
127	  facing = 1;
128	  aiT = 0;               // 通用 AI 计时
129	  state = 0;             // 行为状态
130	  phase = 1;             // Boss 阶段
131	  target: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
132	  squash = 0;            // 史莱姆挤压动画 -1..1
133	  stuckT = 0;            // 飞行怪卡墙计时（脱困用）
134	  stuckCd = 0;           // 脱困后的游荡冷却
135	  jumpStartX = 0;        // 史莱姆本次起跳的 x（落地时判定是否白跳）
136	  chargesLeft = 0;       // EoC 剩余冲撞次数
137	  dashing = false;       // EoC 冲撞中（无视地形）
138	  visAngle = Math.PI;    // EoC 显示角度（平滑追踪移动方向；素材默认朝左）
139	  spin = 0;              // EoC 变身旋转进度 0..1
140	  hpBarT = 0;            // 受击后血条显示计时（tick）
141	  walkCycleT = 0;        // 行走帧累加器（≈原版 frameCounter，按 |vx| 推进）
142	  /** 原版 netID 变种（负数 SetDefaultsFromNetId）：scale/颜色/属性覆盖 */
143	  vanillaScale = 1;
144	  /** 实际生效的负 netID（SetDefaultsFromNetId；凝胶染色过滤用） */
145	  vanillaNetId = 0;
146	  /** 原版 NPC.color 当前值（SetDefaults/SetDefaultsFromNetId 初值，AI_001 逐 tick 渐变）。
147	   *  渲染语义（Main.cs:24527 + NPC.GetColor L94903）：color≠default 时用同贴图二次绘制，
148	   *  逐像素乘 color（贴轮廓）；通道 A 决定该 pass 强度 */
149	  colorRGBA: [number, number, number, number] | null = null;
150	  /** 原版 SetDefaults alpha：每类型静态不透明度基线（渲染 1-alpha/255，NPC.Opacity）。
151	   *  多数为 0=不透明；史莱姆 175/120=半透明凝胶、水母 20、蝙蝠 30 等。
152	   *  无通用渐隐——仅特定家族（幽灵/怨灵等）在自己的 AI 内衰减 */
153	  spawnAlpha = 0;
154	  /** 原版 ai[0] 跳跳计数器（史莱姆 hop 节奏）；ai[2] 卡墙禁追冷却 */
155	  ai0 = -1120;
156	  ai2 = 0;
157	  /** 原版 ai[1]（史莱姆体内物品 id，驱动 SLIME_ITEM_COLORS 渐变色；0=无） */
158	  ai1 = 0;
159	  /** 原版 timeLeft（默认 7500 tick，离屏递减；飘浮眼白天 EncourageDespawn(10)） */
160	  despawnTimer = 7500;
161	  inWater = false;       // 入水检测（溅落声用）
162	
163	  constructor(public key: string, x: number, y: number) {
164	    super();
165	    this.def = ENEMY_DEFS[key] ?? PLACEHOLDER_DEF;
166	    this.hp = this.def.hp;
167	    this.maxHp = this.def.hp;
168	    this.w = this.def.width;
169	    this.h = this.def.height;
170	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
171	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
172	  }
173	
174	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
175	    this.prevX = this.x; this.prevY = this.y;
176	    this.aiT++;
177	    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
178	    if (this.hpBarT > 0) this.hpBarT--;
179	    if (this.squash !== 0) this.squash *= 0.85;
180	    this.animT++;
181	    this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx); // 原版 zombie/fighter 行走帧按 |vx| 累加（L77072/L70216）
182	
183	    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
184	    const hasPlayer = !!player && !player.dead;
185	
186	    // ---- 原版数据驱动路径：按 aiStyle 分发到已移植的 AI 家族（分发后落入共享尾段） ----
187	    // 蠕虫身体段（wormFollow 非空）无 AI：位置由头部 wormAI 沿链驱动，但仍走共享尾段（接触伤害等）
188	    // 小动物（critter）优先走被动游荡，绝不落入 default 的追击 fallback
189	    if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow && this.vanilla.critter) {
190	      this.critterWanderAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null);
191	    } else if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow) {
192	      const p = hasPlayer ? player : null;
193	      switch (this.vanilla.aiStyle) {
194	        case 1: this.slimeAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_001 史莱姆
195	        case 2: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_002 飘浮眼（原版核）
196	        case 3: this.fighterAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_003 战士族（原版通用核）
197	        case 5: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;                         // AI_005 噬魂怪族（原版蜂群核）
198	        case 6: this.wormAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_006 蠕虫族（头）
199	        case 8: this.casterAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_008 法师族（传送+弹幕）
200	        case 14: this.batAI(game, p); break;                            // AI_014 蝙蝠/飞行族（1.4.5.6 原版核）
201	        case 16: this.swimAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_016 游泳族（鲨鱼/食人鱼）
202	        case 18: this.jellyfishAI(game, p); break;                      // AI_018 水母族（脉冲游动）
203	        case 22: this.ghostAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_022 幽灵族（穿墙缓速）
204	        case 26: this.chargerAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_026 独角兽/冲锋族（原版核）
205	        case 107: this.chargerAI(game, p, 1); break;                    // AI_107 ImprovedWalkers（0.07/±1.0 walker 档，NPC.145.cs:43153 引擎核同构）
206	        default: this.zombieAI(game, p); break;                        // 其余家族待逐个移植
207	      }
208	    } else switch (this.key) {
209	      case 'slime_green':
210	      case 'slime_blue': this.slimeAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
211	      case 'zombie': this.zombieAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
212	      case 'demon_eye':
213	      case 'cave_bat': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.09); break;
214	      case 'servant_of_cthulhu': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.22); break;
215	      case 'eye_of_cthulhu': this.eocAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
216	    }
217	
218	    // 入水溅落声（与主角一致）：音量随与主角距离衰减，Boss 更响
219	    const eli = game.world.store.liquid[game.world.store.idx(
220	      Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE),
221	    )];
222	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
223	    this.inWater = eli > 80;
224	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && hasPlayer) {
225	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
226	      const vol = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, 1 - d / (TILE * 45))) * (this.def.boss ? 1.2 : 0.9);
227	      game.playSfx('splash', vol);
228	    }
229	
230	    // 接触伤害（带上攻击者引用，供荆棘反伤）。damage=0（小动物等友好生物）不触发——

```

78	  '-9': { base: 1, scale: 1.2, hp: 45, dmg: 15, def: 7 },   // 黄
79	  '-10': { base: 1, scale: 1.1, hp: 60, dmg: 18, def: 6 },  // 丛林
80	  '-11': { base: 6, scale: 0.85 },   // 小噬魂怪
81	  '-12': { base: 6, scale: 1.15 },   // 大噬魂怪
82	  '-15': { base: 1, scale: 1.15 },   // 史莱姆王子
83	  '-22': { base: 223, scale: 1.0 }, '-23': { base: 223, scale: 1.0 },
84	  '-24': { base: 223, scale: 1.0 }, '-25': { base: 223, scale: 1.0 },
85	  // 僵尸/骷髅/眼变种 = 基底 + scale（贴图同基底，属性缩放）
86	  '-38': { base: 3, scale: 0.85 }, '-39': { base: 3, scale: 0.85 }, '-40': { base: 3, scale: 0.85 },
87	  '-41': { base: 3, scale: 0.85 }, '-42': { base: 3, scale: 0.85 },
88	  '-43': { base: 2, scale: 0.85 },  // 小恶魔眼
89	  '-46': { base: 21, scale: 0.9 }, '-47': { base: 21, scale: 0.9 },
90	  '-48': { base: 201, scale: 0.9 }, '-49': { base: 201, scale: 0.9 },
91	  '-50': { base: 202, scale: 0.9 }, '-51': { base: 202, scale: 0.9 },
92	  '-52': { base: 203, scale: 0.9 }, '-53': { base: 203, scale: 0.9 },
93	  '-54': { base: 223, scale: 0.9 }, '-55': { base: 223, scale: 0.9 },
94	};
95	
96	export class VanillaSpawner {
97	  // ---- SpawnFlags（Spawner 字段 L39-137） ----
98	  private pX = 0; private pY = 0;
99	  private dayTime = true;
100	  private hardMode = false;
101	  private waterTile = false;
102	  private noWorms = false;         // 原版 wallHouse（房屋内不出蠕虫）
103	  private skyMob = false;
104	  private surfaceSpawn = false;
105	  private underGround = false;      // 原 underGround = worldSurface < y < rockLayer
106	  private deeperThanRockLayer = false;
107	  private isOcean = false;
108	  private isBeach = false;
109	  private nearMarble = false;
110	  private nearGranite = false;
111	  private spawnUndergroundDesert = false;
112	  private ZoneSnow = false; private ZoneCorrupt = false; private ZoneCrimson = false;
113	  private ZoneHallow = false; private ZoneJungle = false; private ZoneGlowshroom = false;
114	  private ZoneDungeon = false; private ZoneGraveyard = false; private ZoneBeach = false;
115	  private spawnTileX = 0; private spawnTileY = 0;
116	  /** FindSpawnTile 的 xRange 输出（L911：落脚点横向在 safeArea 内，传给 SpawnAnNPC） */
117	  private xRange = false;
118	  private spawnTileType = 0;
119	  /** 落脚点（Game 放置用） */
120	  currentSpawnX = 0;
121	  currentSpawnY = 0;
122	
123	  constructor(private world: World) {}
124	
125	  /** 造怪入口：netId 可为负（SetDefaultsFromNetId 映射） */
126	  private spawnNPC(x: number, y: number, netId: number, rng: RNG): Enemy | null {
127	    const map = NET_ID_MAP[netId];
128	    const baseId = map?.base ?? netId;
129	    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(baseId, x, y);
130	    if (!e) return null;
131	    // 原版 NewNPC 锚点(NPC.cs:46596-46597):X-width/2, Y-height(底部贴格顶)。
132	    // 此前 fromVanilla 中心锚(y-h/2)把怪埋进落脚实心格半格高——飞行怪沿地面内沿
133	    // 漂移穿过侧壁,看起来像穿墙(噬魂怪等 ai5 族)
134	    e.y = y - e.h;
135	    const ov = NET_ID_OVERRIDE[String(netId)];
136	    e.vanillaNetId = netId;
137	    if (map) {
138	      e.vanillaScale = ov?.scale ?? map.scale;   // scale 优先源数据（SetDefaults_ForNetId 第 3 参）
139	      if (map.hp != null) e.hp = e.maxHp = map.hp;
140	      if (map.dmg != null) e.def.damage = map.dmg;
141	      if (map.def != null) e.def.defense = map.def;
142	    } else if (ov?.scale != null) {
143	      e.vanillaScale = ov.scale;
144	    }
145	    // SetDefaultsFromNetId 覆盖 color/alpha（L7633+：color = new Color(...) / alpha = N）
146	    if (ov?.color) e.colorRGBA = [ov.color[0], ov.color[1], ov.color[2], ov.color[3] ?? 255];
147	    if (ov?.alpha != null) e.spawnAlpha = ov.alpha;
148	    e.id = this.world.store.w; // 占位，Game 侧会重编
149	    return e;
150	  }
151	
152	  // ---- SetSpawnFlagsForChosenTile（L950-1185） ----
153	  private setFlagsForChosenTile(spawnTileX: number, spawnTileY: number, spawnTileType: number): void {
154	    const st = this.world.store;
155	    this.spawnTileX = spawnTileX; this.spawnTileY = spawnTileY; this.spawnTileType = spawnTileType;
156	    // waterTile（L957）：落脚格上方两格都是液体且为水
157	    const above1 = st.idx(spawnTileX, spawnTileY - 1), above2 = st.idx(spawnTileX, spawnTileY - 2);
158	    this.waterTile = st.liquid[above1] > 0 && st.liquid[above2] > 0 && st.liquidType[above1] === 1;
159	    // nearMarble/nearGranite（L958-1006）：tile 367/368 或玩家处
160	    if (spawnTileType === T.MARBLE) this.nearMarble = true;
161	    else if (spawnTileType === T.GRANITE) this.nearGranite = true;
162	    else if (st.type[st.idx(this.pX, this.pY)] === T.MARBLE) this.nearMarble = true;
163	    else if (st.type[st.idx(this.pX, this.pY)] === T.GRANITE) this.nearGranite = true;
164	    // 地下沙漠（L1077-1100）：墙 216/217（ AllowsUndergroundDesertEnemiesToSpawn）
165	    const w = st.wall[st.idx(spawnTileX, spawnTileY)];
166	    if (spawnTileY < this.world.rockLevel && spawnTileY > 200) {
167	      this.spawnUndergroundDesert = w === 216 || w === 217 || w === 187;
168	    }
169	    this.surfaceSpawn = spawnTileY <= this.world.groundLevel;
170	    this.deeperThanRockLayer = spawnTileY >= this.world.rockLevel;
171	    this.underGround = spawnTileY > this.world.groundLevel && spawnTileY < this.world.rockLevel;
172	    // isOcean（L1104）：边缘 + 沙 tile + 岩石层上
173	    this.isOcean = (spawnTileX < 250 || spawnTileX > st.w - 250) && spawnTileType === T.SAND && spawnTileY < this.world.rockLevel;
174	    this.isBeach = spawnTileY <= this.world.groundLevel && (spawnTileX < 340 || spawnTileX > st.w - 340);
175	    // Zone 判定（SceneMetrics.cs:16/613-615/678）：以玩家为中心 169×123 窗口计数 ≥300。
176	    // 旧实现只看落脚格类型且漏黑檀石 25——腐化地表大量铺黑檀石导致噬魂怪永不触发
177	    this.ZoneSnow = spawnTileType === T.SNOW || spawnTileType === T.ICE || spawnTileType === T.SNOW_BRICK;
178	    this.ZoneCorrupt = this.countTiles(EVIL_LOOKUP, this.pX, this.pY) >= 300;
179	    this.ZoneCrimson = this.countTiles(BLOOD_LOOKUP, this.pX, this.pY) >= 300;
180	    this.ZoneHallow = spawnTileType === T.PEARLSAND || spawnTileType === T.HOLLOW_ICE;
181	    this.ZoneJungle = spawnTileType === T.MUD || spawnTileType === T.JUNGLE_GRASS;
182	    this.ZoneGlowshroom = spawnTileType === T.MUSHROOM_GRASS;
183	    this.ZoneDungeon = spawnTileType === T.DUNGEON_BLUE;
184	  }
185	
186	  /** SceneMetrics.ScanTiles 近似:中心 169×123 窗口(zoneScanSize=120×75+padding 25×2−1)计数 */
187	  private countTiles(lookup: Uint8Array, cx: number, cy: number): number {
188	    const st = this.world.store;
189	    const x0 = Math.max(2, cx - 84), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 3, cx + 84);
190	    const y0 = Math.max(2, cy - 61), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 3, cy + 61);
191	    let n = 0;
192	    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
193	      const row = y * st.w;
194	      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
195	        const t = st.type[row + x];
196	        if (lookup[t]) n++;
197	        else if (t === T.SUNFLOWER) n -= 10; // 向日葵每株 −10(SceneMetrics.cs:588 num=-10)
198	      }
199	    }
200	    return n;
201	  }
202	
203	  // ---- FindSpawnTile（L879-920）----
204	  /** FindSpawnTile（L879-927）+ GetSpawnArea（L841-877）1:1：
205	   *  spawnArea = 玩家 ±(0.7×全屏格)（均匀取点，不是环带外扩！），
206	   *  safeArea = 玩家 ±(0.52×全屏格)——生成点必须落在画面外（!safeArea.Contains）。
207	   *  viewHalfW/H = 半屏格数 → 0.7×全屏 = ×1.4 半屏；0.52×全屏 = ×1.04 半屏 */
208	  private findSpawnTile(rng: RNG, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): boolean {
209	    const st = this.world.store;
210	    const px = this.pX, py = this.pY;
211	    const rangeX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfW * 1.4));
212	    const rangeY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfH * 1.4));
213	    const safeX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfW * 1.04));
214	    const safeY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfH * 1.04));
215	    // WorldUtils.ClampToWorld
216	    const areaL = Math.max(2, px - rangeX), areaR = Math.min(st.w - 3, px + rangeX);
217	    const areaT = Math.max(2, py - rangeY), areaB = Math.min(st.h - 3, py + rangeY);
218	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
219	      const tx = rng.int(areaL, areaR);
220	      let ty = rng.int(areaT, areaB);
221	      // L886-888：点在实心格 → 重试（wallHouse 房屋墙检查我们无房屋墙数据，略）
222	      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
223	      this.skyMob = false;
224	      // L890-897：天空怪——高于 worldSurface×0.35 且在世界两侧 45% 之外（肉前非 hardMode 分支）
225	      if (ty < this.world.groundLevel * 0.35 && (tx < st.w * 0.45 || tx > st.w * 0.55)) {
226	        this.skyMob = true;
227	      } else {
228	        // L900-906：向下找第一个实心格 = 落脚面（扫描上限 = spawnArea.Bottom，非世界底）
229	        let j = ty;
230	        while (j < areaB && !st.isSolid(tx, j)) j++;
231	        if (j >= areaB) continue;
232	        ty = j;
233	      }
234	      // L910：safeArea 内（画面内）一律拒绝 + HasTileSpawnSpace 落点空间校验
235	      if (Math.abs(tx - px) < safeX && Math.abs(ty - py) < safeY) continue;
236	      if (!this.hasTileSpawnSpace(tx, ty)) continue;
237	      // L911：xRange = 落脚点在 safeArea 横向范围内（原版传给 SpawnAnNPC 的水生成判定）
238	      this.xRange = tx >= px - safeX && tx < px + safeX;
239	      this.spawnTileX = tx; this.spawnTileY = ty;
240	      // 落脚处 tile type（原版 GetProperGroundSpawnTileTypeAndWallType）
241	      const groundType = this.skyMob ? 0 : st.type[st.idx(tx, ty)];
242	      this.setFlagsForChosenTile(tx, ty, groundType);
243	      return true;
244	    }
245	    return false;
246	  }
247	
248	  /** HasTileSpawnSpace（L5306+5330）：落脚格上方 spawnSpaceX=2 × spawnSpaceY=3 区域
249	   *  逐格 CanSpawnInTile——非实心且非岩浆（防刷进实心/岩浆里） */
250	  private hasTileSpawnSpace(tx: number, ty: number): boolean {
251	    const st = this.world.store;
252	    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 0; dx++) {
253	      for (let dy = -3; dy <= -1; dy++) {
254	        const x = tx + dx, y = ty + dy;
255	        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
256	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
257	        if (st.isSolid(x, y)) return false;
258	        if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 2) return false;
259	      }
260	    }
261	    return true;
262	  }
263	
264	  /** CheckNotSpawningOnScreen（L5344-5366）像素级双保险：
265	   *  落脚格 16×16 与"玩家屏幕 ±safeRange"矩形相交 → 拒绝。
266	   *  注意原版单位混用（L5354）：safeRangeX/Y 是格数但直接当像素偏移加——实际外扩仅 ~2 格 */
267	  private checkNotSpawningOnScreen(px: number, py: number, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): boolean {
268	    const sW = viewHalfW * 2 * TILE, sH = viewHalfH * 2 * TILE;
269	    const safePxX = viewHalfW * 1.04, safePxY = viewHalfH * 1.04; // 格数直接当像素（原版 L5354 原样）
270	    const rectX = px - sW / 2 - safePxX, rectY = py - sH / 2 - safePxY;
271	    const rectW = sW + safePxX * 2, rectH = sH + safePxY * 2;
272	    const tX = this.spawnTileX * TILE, tY = this.spawnTileY * TILE;
273	    return !(tX < rectX + rectW && tX + TILE > rectX && tY < rectY + rectH && tY + TILE > rectY);
274	  }
275	
276	  // ---- SpawnAnNPC（L1186-5144）——肉前分支 1:1，hardMode 分支保留 ----
277	  private spawnAnNPC(rng: RNG): Enemy | null {
278	    const st = this.world.store;
279	    const x = this.spawnTileX * TILE + 8;
280	    const y = this.spawnTileY * TILE;
281	    const N = (n: number) => rng.next() < 1 / n;  // Main.rand.Next(n)==0
282	    const hardMode = this.hardMode;
283	    const t = this.spawnTileType;
284	    const D = (id: number) => this.spawnNPC(x, y, id, rng);
285	
286	    // ---- 蜘蛛巢（L1569-1587）：墙 62 ----
287	    const wall = st.wall[st.idx(this.spawnTileX, this.spawnTileY)];
288	    if (wall === 62) {
289	      if (N(10)) return D(163);  // hardMode 蜘蛛；肉前爬行者
290	      return D(164);
291	    }
292	    // ---- 地下沙漠（L1589-1672）----
293	    if (this.spawnUndergroundDesert) {
294	      if (N(15)) return D(537);
295	      const r = rng.next();
296	      if (r < 0.5) return D(580);
297	      if (r < 0.9) return D(581);
298	      return D(69);
299	    }
300	    // ---- 海洋（L1705-1834）----
301	    if (this.waterTile && this.isOcean) {
302	      if (N(10)) return D(220);
303	      if (N(18)) return D(221);
304	      if (N(3)) return D(67);   // 螃蟹
305	      return D(64);             // 默认粉水母
306	    }
307	    // ---- 水池段（L1839-1905）----
308	    if (this.waterTile && !this.isOcean) {
309	      // 原版水池在地下也是这些
310	      if (N(6)) return D(63);   // 蓝水母
311	      return D(63);
312	    }
313	    // ---- 小动物（spawnFriendly 段 L2006-2535，白天 + 草/土 tile + Next(15) 门）----
314	    if (this.dayTime && !this.waterTile && this.surfaceSpawn &&
315	      (t === T.GRASS || t === T.DIRT || t === T.SNOW || t === T.SNOW_BRICK || t === 477)) {
316	      if (N(15)) {
317	        if (t === T.SNOW || t === T.SNOW_BRICK) {
318	          // 雪原小动物（L148→148|149 Next(2)）
319	          if (N(2)) return D(148);
320	          return D(149);
321	        }
322	        // 森林小动物概率表（原版 butterflyChance/stinkBugChance 动态值，取代表值）
323	        const r = rng.next();
324	        if (r < 0.2) return D(357);       // 蚯蚓
325	        if (r < 0.45) return D(377);      // 蚱蜢
326	        if (r < 0.65) return D(46);       // 兔子
327	        if (r < 0.8) return D(299);       // 松鼠
328	        if (r < 0.9) return D(300);       // 老鼠
329	        return D(74);                     // 鸟
330	      }
331	    }
332	    // ---- 蘑菇地（L3540-3610，tile 70）----
333	    if (t === T.MUSHROOM_GRASS) {
334	      if (this.surfaceSpawn) {
335	        if (N(3)) {
336	          if (N(4)) return D(259);
337	          return D(257);
338	        }
339	        return D(254);
340	      }
341	      if (N(8)) return D(360);
342	      if (N(4)) return D(259);
343	      return D(257);
344	    }
345	    // ---- 丛林地表（L3713-3740）----
346	    if (t === T.JUNGLE_GRASS) {
347	      if (N(2)) {
348	        // 丛林地表池
349	        if (N(3)) return D(158);
350	        return D(51);
351	      }
352	      return D(51);  // SpawnHornet
353	    }
354	    // ---- 沙漠地表（L3859-3928，沙尘暴外简化为沙漠 tile 段）----
355	    if (t === T.SAND || t === T.HARDENED_SAND || t === T.SANDSTONE) {
356	      if (!hardMode) {
357	        if (N(6)) return D(69);   // 蚁狮
358	        return D(61);             // 秃鹫（地表沙）
359	      }
360	    }
361	    // ---- 猩红（L3973-4031）----
362	    if (this.ZoneCrimson) {
363	      if (N(5)) return D(182);
364	      if (N(2)) return D(268);
365	      if (N(2)) return D(181);
366	      return D(173);  // 默认 Crimera
367	    }
368	    // ---- 腐化（L4032-4074）----
369	    if (this.ZoneCorrupt) {
370	      if (N(3)) return D(101);
371	      if (N(3)) return D(-11);   // 小噬魂怪
372	      if (N(3)) return D(-12);   // 大噬魂怪
373	      return D(6);               // 默认 Eater of Souls
374	    }
375	    // ---- 地表（L4075-4717）----
376	    if (this.surfaceSpawn) {
377	      if (this.ZoneSnow) {
378	        // 雪原地表（L4560+）
379	        if (hardMode && N(3)) return D(155);
380	        if (N(6)) return D(147);   // 冰史莱姆
381	        return D(161);             // 雪原狼
382	      }
383	      if (this.dayTime) {
384	        // 白天地表小动物门已过 → 池底
385	        if (!this.waterTile) {
386	          return D(this.getBasicSlimeToSpawn(true, t, rng));  // L4402
387	        }
388	        return null;
389	      }
390	      // 夜晚（L4454-4716）：Next(6) 门
391	      if (N(6) || (this.world.clock.dayCount >= 0 && N(2) && false)) {  // moonPhase 暂无
392	        if (N(2)) {
393	          if (N(4)) return D(-43);  // 小恶魔眼
394	          return D(2);              // 恶魔眼
395	        }
396	        // switch(Next(5)) 僵尸系
397	        const zv = rng.int(0, 5);
398	        const zm = [190, 191, 192, 193, 194][zv] ?? 3;
399	        if (N(3)) {
400	          // 各配 1/3 概率小变种
401	          const small = [-38, -39, -40, -41, -42][zv] ?? -38;
402	          return D(small);
403	        }
404	        return D(zm);
405	      }
406	      // 夜间池底（L4561+ torch zombie 等）
407	      if (!this.ZoneSnow && !this.ZoneJungle && !this.waterTile) {
408	        return D(3);  // 普通僵尸兜底
409	      }
410	      return null;
411	    }
412	    // ---- 地下层（L4718-4770，worldSurface < y < rockLayer）----
413	    if (this.underGround) {
414	      if (!this.noWorms && N(50) && !this.ZoneSnow) {
415	        return D(10);   // Giant Worm
416	      }
417	      if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(147);
418	      return D(this.getBasicSlimeToSpawn(false, t, rng));
419	    }
420	    // ---- 地狱（L4771-4820，y > maxTilesY-190）----
421	    if (this.spawnTileY > st.h - 190) {
422	      if (N(40)) return D(39);   // Bone Serpent
423	      if (N(14)) return D(24);   // Fire Imp
424	      if (N(7)) {
425	        if (N(10)) return D(66);  // Voodoo Demon
426	        return D(62);             // Demon
427	      }
428	      if (N(3)) return D(59);    // Lava Slime
429	      return D(60);              // Hellbat
430	    }
431	    // ---- 洞穴通用池（L4825-5142）----
432	    if (N(60)) {
433	      if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(218);
434	      return D(217);
435	    }
436	    if (!this.noWorms && N(100) && !this.ZoneHallow) {
437	      if (!hardMode) {
438	        if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(185);
439	        return D(10);
440	      }
441	    }
442	    if (this.ZoneSnow && N(20)) return D(185);
443	    if ((!hardMode && N(10)) || (hardMode && N(20))) {
444	      if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(184);
445	      if (N(3)) return D(-6);  // 黑史莱姆
446	      return D(16);            // 母史莱姆
447	    }
448	    if ((!hardMode) && N(4)) {
449	      if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(184);
450	      if (N(3)) return D(-6);
451	      return D(184);
452	    }
453	    if (N(2)) {
454	      // 洞穴主池（L4896+）
455	      if (N(35) && !this.waterTile) return D(453);  // Creeper Egg
456	      if (N(80)) return D(195);                     // Tim（罕）
457	      if (N(200)) return D(45);                     // Bound Goblin
458	      if (this.nearMarble && !N(4)) {
459	        if (hardMode && N(6)) return D(480);
460	        return D(481);
461	      }
462	      if (this.nearGranite && !N(5)) {
463	        if (N(6)) return D(483);
464	        return D(482);
465	      }
466	      if (N(20)) return D(44);   // 附魔剑
467	      // switch(Next(4)) 骷髅系
468	      const sv = rng.int(0, 4);
469	      const sm = [21, 201, 202, 203][sv] ?? 21;
470	      if (N(3)) {
471	        const small = [[-46, -47], [-48, -49], [-50, -51], [-52, -53]][sv] ?? [-46, -47];
472	        return D(rng.next() < 0.5 ? small[0] : small[1]);
473	      }
474	      return D(sm);
475	    }
476	    // 洞穴兜底（L5101-5142）
477	    if (this.ZoneJungle) return D(51);
478	    if (this.ZoneSnow) return D(150);
479	    return D(49);  // Cave Bat
480	
481	    return null;
482	  }
483	
484	  /** GetBasicSlimeToSpawn（L5537-5580） */
485	  private getBasicSlimeToSpawn(surface: boolean, tileType: number, rng: RNG): number {
486	    if (!surface) {
487	      // 地下：Next(5)==0→-9（黄），否则 Next(2)==0→1（蓝）/-8（红）
488	      if (rng.next() < 0.2) return -9;
489	      return rng.next() < 0.5 ? 1 : -8;
490	    }
491	    switch (tileType) {
492	      case T.MUD: return -10;     // 丛林
493	      case T.SNOW: case T.SNOW_BRICK: return 147;  // 冰
494	      default:
495	        if (rng.next() < 1 / 3) return -3;         // 绿
496	        if (rng.next() < 0.1) return -7;           // 紫
497	        return 1;                                   // 蓝
498	    }
499	  }
500	
501	  /** 主入口：Game.trySpawnEnemy 调用 */
502	  spawn(playerX: number, playerY: number, rng: RNG, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): Enemy | null {
503	    this.pX = Math.floor(playerX / TILE);
504	    this.pY = Math.floor(playerY / TILE);
505	    this.dayTime = this.world.clock.isDay;
506	    this.hardMode = !!this.world.flags.hardMode;
507	    this.noWorms = false;
508	    this.skyMob = false;
509	    if (!this.findSpawnTile(rng, viewHalfW, viewHalfH)) return null;
510	    // TrySpawnAnNPC L220：CheckNotSpawningOnScreen 像素级二次校验（画面内/贴屏拒绝）
511	    if (!this.checkNotSpawningOnScreen(playerX, playerY, viewHalfW, viewHalfH)) return null;
512	    this.currentSpawnX = this.spawnTileX;
513	    this.currentSpawnY = this.spawnTileY;
514	    // 探针调试覆盖：debugPoolOverride 非空时绕过原版链直接从池选（确定性验证）
515	    if (debugPoolOverride && debugPoolOverride.length) {
516	      return this.spawnNPC(this.spawnTileX * 16 + 8, this.spawnTileY * 16,
517	        debugPoolOverride[rng.int(0, debugPoolOverride.length - 1)], rng);
518	    }
519	    return this.spawnAnNPC(rng);
520	  }
521	}
522

VanillaSpawner: `countTiles` scans 169×123 = ~20,787 tiles, and it's called TWICE (ZoneCorrupt + ZoneCrimson) per setFlagsForChosenTile call — and setFlagsForChosenTile is called per attempt (up to 50 attempts in findSpawnTile) within each spawn() call. Wait — no: setFlagsForChosenTile is called only when an attempt succeeds (line 242, after return true). Actually findSpawnTile loops up to 50 attempts; each attempt that passes the solid/space checks calls setFlagsForChosenTile and returns true immediately. So countTiles is called 2× per spawn() call (once, on success). But wait — setFlagsForChosenTile calls countTiles twice = 2 × 20,787 = ~41.6K tile reads per spawn call. Spawn calls happen every SPAWN_CHECK_INTERVAL=30 ticks (0.5s) when enemies below cap. That's the SceneMetrics-style counting. But note: Game.scene already has zoneCorrupt etc. computed every 15 ticks via scanScene — so this is a duplicate computation.

Let me check scanScene in SceneMetrics.ts to quantify.

Also check where spawn() is called in Game.ts (trySpawnEnemy ~line 2029).

Then WldImport and Renderer particles. Let me read those.

86	/** 以玩家中心扫描并计算全部 Zone 标志（对应 ScanAndApplyToPlayer 主链） */
87	export function scanScene(
88	  world: World, centerX: number, centerY: number, townNPCCount: number,
89	): SceneFlags {
90	  const st = world.store;
91	  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
92	  const underworldLayer = world.lavaLine || (h - 200); // Main.UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200
93	  const worldSurface = world.groundLevel;
94	  const rockLevel = world.rockLevel;
95	  const tileX = Math.floor(centerX / 16);
96	  const tileY = Math.floor(centerY / 16);
97	
98	  // 169×124 采样窗口（SceneMetrics.ZoneScanSize）
99	  const sx0 = Math.max(0, tileX - 84), sx1 = Math.min(w - 1, tileX + 84);
100	  const sy0 = Math.max(0, tileY - 61), sy1 = Math.min(h - 1, tileY + 62);
101	  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
102	  let sunflower = 0;
103	  for (let y = sy0; y <= sy1; y++) {
104	    for (let x = sx0; x <= sx1; x++) {
105	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
106	      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
107	      const t = st.type[i];
108	      counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
109	      if (t === SUNFLOWER) sunflower++;
110	    }
111	  }
112	
113	  // 聚合（AggregateTileCounts L603-670：含神圣/邪恶/猩红互减、向日葵 -10）
114	  let holy = countSet(counts, HOLY_TILES);
115	  let evil = countSet(counts, EVIL_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
116	  let blood = countSet(counts, BLOOD_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
117	  const holyRaw = holy;
118	  holy -= evil; holy -= blood;
119	  evil -= holyRaw; blood -= holyRaw;
120	  holy = Math.max(0, holy); evil = Math.max(0, evil); blood = Math.max(0, blood);
121	
122	  const jungle = countSet(counts, JUNGLE_TILES);
123	  const snow = countSet(counts, SNOW_TILES);
124	  const mushroom = countSet(counts, MUSHROOM_TILES);
125	  const meteor = countSet(counts, METEOR_TILES);
126	  const dungeonTiles = countSet(counts, DUNGEON_TILES);
127	  const desert = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, SAND_TILES));
128	  const graveyard = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, GRAVEYARD_TILES) - (sunflower >> 1));
129	
130	  // Zone 判定（CalculateZones L673-697）
131	  const zoneUnderworldHeight = tileY > underworldLayer;
132	  const belowSurface = tileY > worldSurface;
133	  const centerWall = st.inBounds(tileX, tileY) ? st.wall[st.idx(tileX, tileY)] : 0;
134	  const zoneDungeon = dungeonTiles >= DUNGEON_THRESHOLD && belowSurface && DUNGEON_WALLS.has(centerWall);
135	  const zoneDesert = desert >= DESERT_THRESHOLD;
136	
137	  // oceanDepths（WorldGen.cs:4390-4400：y <= (worldSurface+rockLayer)/2+40 且 x 距边 < beachDistance）
138	  const beachDistance = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06));
139	  const oceanTop = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
140	  const zoneBeach = tileY <= oceanTop && (tileX < beachDistance || tileX > w - beachDistance);
141	
142	  return {
143	    tileX, tileY,
144	    belowSurface,
145	    zoneSkyHeight: tileY <= worldSurface * 0.35,
146	    zoneOverworldHeight: tileY <= worldSurface && tileY > worldSurface * 0.35,
147	    zoneDirtLayerHeight: tileY <= rockLevel && tileY > worldSurface,
148	    zoneRockLayerHeight: tileY <= underworldLayer && tileY > rockLevel,
149	    zoneUnderworldHeight,
150	    zoneCorrupt: evil >= CORRUPTION_THRESHOLD,
151	    zoneCrimson: blood >= CRIMSON_THRESHOLD,
152	    zoneHallow: holy >= HALLOW_THRESHOLD,
153	    zoneJungle: jungle >= JUNGLE_THRESHOLD && !zoneUnderworldHeight,
154	    zoneSnow: snow >= SNOW_THRESHOLD,
155	    zoneDesert,
156	    zoneGlowshroom: mushroom >= MUSHROOM_THRESHOLD,
157	    zoneMeteor: meteor >= METEOR_THRESHOLD,
158	    zoneGraveyard: graveyard >= GRAVEYARD_THRESHOLD,
159	    zoneDungeon,
160	    zoneTemple: centerWall === 87,
161	    zoneBeach,
162	    zoneUndergroundDesert: zoneDesert && belowSurface && SANDSTONE_WALLS.has(centerWall),
163	    townNPCCount,
164	  };
165	}
166

1	// 小端二进制读取器（.NET BinaryReader 兼容）
2	export class BinaryReaderLE {
3	  pos = 0;
4	  constructor(public buf: Uint8Array) {}
5	  get eof() { return this.pos >= this.buf.length; }
6	
7	  byte(): number { return this.buf[this.pos++]; }
8	  u8(): number { return this.buf[this.pos++]; }
9	  i8(): number { return (this.buf[this.pos++] << 24) >> 24; }
10	  i16(): number {
11	    const v = this.buf[this.pos] | (this.buf[this.pos + 1] << 8);
12	    this.pos += 2;
13	    return (v << 16) >> 16;
14	  }
15	  u16(): number {
16	    const v = this.buf[this.pos] | (this.buf[this.pos + 1] << 8);
17	    this.pos += 2;
18	    return v;
19	  }
20	  i32(): number {
21	    const v = (this.buf[this.pos] | (this.buf[this.pos + 1] << 8) |
22	      (this.buf[this.pos + 2] << 16) | (this.buf[this.pos + 3] << 24)) >>> 0;
23	    this.pos += 4;
24	    return v | 0;
25	  }
26	  u32(): number {
27	    const v = (this.buf[this.pos] | (this.buf[this.pos + 1] << 8) |
28	      (this.buf[this.pos + 2] << 16) | (this.buf[this.pos + 3] << 24)) >>> 0;
29	    this.pos += 4;
30	    return v;
31	  }
32	  i64(): bigint {
33	    const lo = this.u32(), hi = this.u32();
34	    return (BigInt(hi) << 32n) | BigInt(lo);
35	  }
36	  u64(): bigint {
37	    return BigInt.asUintN(64, this.i64());
38	  }
39	  f32(): number {
40	    const v = this.u32();
41	    const dv = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(4));
42	    dv.setUint32(0, v, true);
43	    return dv.getFloat32(0, true);
44	  }
45	  f64(): number {
46	    const dv = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(8));
47	    dv.setUint32(0, this.u32(), true);
48	    dv.setUint32(4, this.u32(), true);
49	    return dv.getFloat64(0, true);
50	  }
51	  boolean(): boolean { return this.buf[this.pos++] !== 0; }
52	  bytes(n: number): Uint8Array {
53	    const v = this.buf.subarray(this.pos, this.pos + n);
54	    this.pos += n;
55	    return v;
56	  }
57	  /** .NET BinaryReader.ReadString：7-bit varint 长度 + UTF-8 */
58	  string(): string {
59	    let len = 0, shift = 0;
60	    for (;;) {
61	      const b = this.buf[this.pos++];
62	      len |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
63	      if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
64	      shift += 7;
65	    }
66	    const bytes = this.bytes(len);
67	    return new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes);
68	  }
69	  seek(p: number) { this.pos = p; }
70	}
